function handleLatLonPosition(pos) {
	var requestURL = '/api/internalapi/?action=setLatLon&lat=' + pos.coords.latitude + '&lng=' + pos.coords.longitude;
	console.log('API: ' + requestURL);

	var request = $.ajax({
    url: requestURL,
    type: "get",
		async: false,
    data: {}
  });

  request.done(function(response, textStatus, jqXHR){
    console.log('Lat/Lon Stored');
  });
}

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.relative').forEach(function(alert) {
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      alert.classList.remove('hidden');
      alert.classList.add('block');
    });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.close-alert').forEach(function(button) {
    button.addEventListener('click', function() {
      const alertDiv = button.closest('.relative');
      if (alertDiv) {
        alertDiv.classList.remove('block');
        alertDiv.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {

      const nav = document.getElementById('mobileSliderNav');
      if (!nav) return;

      const items = Array.from(nav.querySelectorAll('.group-nav-item'));
      const sections = items.map(btn => document.getElementById(btn.dataset.target));

      let suppressNavClick = false;

      // Expose to inline onclick
      window.navigateTo = function(url) {
        if (suppressNavClick) return;
        window.location.href = url;
      };

      function setActive(activeIdx) {
        items.forEach((btn, i) => {
          btn.classList.toggle('active', i === activeIdx);
        });
      }

      function centerNavItem(btn, behavior = 'smooth') {
        const desired =
          btn.offsetLeft + (btn.offsetWidth / 2) - (nav.clientWidth / 2);

        const maxScroll = nav.scrollWidth - nav.clientWidth;
        const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(desired, maxScroll));

        nav.scrollTo({
          left: clamped,
          behavior
        });
      }

      // Center active item on load
      const initiallyActive = nav.querySelector('.group-nav-item.active');
      if (initiallyActive) centerNavItem(initiallyActive, 'auto');

      // --- Drag to scroll ---
      (function enableDragScroll(container) {

        let isDown = false;
        let startX = 0;
        let startScrollLeft = 0;
        let dragging = false;

        const DRAG_THRESHOLD = 10;

        container.style.cursor = 'grab';

        container.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
          if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && e.button !== 0) return;

          isDown = true;
          dragging = false;
          startX = e.clientX;
          startScrollLeft = container.scrollLeft;

          container.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
          if (!isDown) return;

          const dx = e.clientX - startX;

          if (!dragging && Math.abs(dx) > DRAG_THRESHOLD) {
            dragging = true;
          }

          if (dragging) {
            if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
            container.scrollLeft = startScrollLeft - dx;
          }

        }, { passive: false });

        container.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';

          if (dragging) {
            suppressNavClick = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
              suppressNavClick = false;
            }, 0);
          }
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';
        });

      })(nav);

    });
  </script>

<script>
  function clearFIlters() {
    const clearFiltersButton = document.getElementById('clearFilters');
    clearFiltersButton.click();
  }
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    var showcase = new Splide('#showcase', {
      type: 'loop',
      perPage: 1.2,
      gap: '20px',
      focus: 'center',
      arrows: false,
      pagination: false,
      trimSpace: false,
      autoHeight: true,
      clones: 2,
      // Auto scroll
      autoplay: true,
      interval: 5000,
      pauseOnHover: true,
      pauseOnFocus: true,
    });
    showcase.mount();

    var eventShowcase = new Splide('#eventShowcase', {
      type: 'slide',
      perPage: 2.2,
      gap: '20px',
      focus: 'left',
      arrows: false,
      pagination: false,
      trimSpace: false,
      autoHeight: true,
      clones: 2,
      breakpoints: {
        1280: {
          perPage: 2.2,
        },
        964: {
          perPage: 1.2,
        }
      }
    });
    eventShowcase.mount();
  });

  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
    const searchBar = document.querySelector("#filterControlls-search");
    const filterControls = document.querySelector("#filterControlls");
    window.addEventListener("scroll", function() {
      let filterTop = filterControls.getBoundingClientRect().top;
      // searchBar.classList.remove("hidden");
      if (filterTop <= 35) {
        // searchBar.classList.add("hidden");
      }
    });
  });
</script>
<script>
document.querySelectorAll('.drag-scroll').forEach((slider) => {
  let isDown = false;
  let isDragging = false;
  let startX = 0;
  let scrollLeft = 0;
  const dragThreshold = 6;

  // Desktop mouse drag only
  slider.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
    if (e.button !== 0) return;

    isDown = true;
    isDragging = false;
    startX = e.pageX;
    scrollLeft = slider.scrollLeft;

    slider.classList.add('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
  });

  window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
    if (!isDown) return;

    const dx = e.pageX - startX;

    if (Math.abs(dx) > dragThreshold) {
      isDragging = true;
    }

    if (isDragging) {
      e.preventDefault();
      slider.scrollLeft = scrollLeft - dx;
    }
  });

  slider.querySelectorAll('a').forEach((link) => {
    link.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (isDragging) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
      }
    });

    link.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
    });
  });
});
</script>

<!-- If the developer is debugging in Chrome -->
<script>
GlobalDebuggingInChrome = false;



//Clears Footer Contact Select dropdown
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');
    cinemaSelect.selectedIndex = 0;
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');

  if (!cinemaSelect) return;

  cinemaSelect.addEventListener('change', function () {
    const siteCode = this.value;

    if (siteCode !== '') {
      window.location.href = '/company/contact?site=' + encodeURIComponent(siteCode);
    }
  });
});
</script>

function handleLatLonPosition(pos) {
	var requestURL = '/api/internalapi/?action=setLatLon&lat=' + pos.coords.latitude + '&lng=' + pos.coords.longitude;
	console.log('API: ' + requestURL);

	var request = $.ajax({
    url: requestURL,
    type: "get",
		async: false,
    data: {}
  });

  request.done(function(response, textStatus, jqXHR){
    console.log('Lat/Lon Stored');
  });
}

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.relative').forEach(function(alert) {
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      alert.classList.remove('hidden');
      alert.classList.add('block');
    });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.close-alert').forEach(function(button) {
    button.addEventListener('click', function() {
      const alertDiv = button.closest('.relative');
      if (alertDiv) {
        alertDiv.classList.remove('block');
        alertDiv.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {

      const nav = document.getElementById('mobileSliderNav');
      if (!nav) return;

      const items = Array.from(nav.querySelectorAll('.group-nav-item'));
      const sections = items.map(btn => document.getElementById(btn.dataset.target));

      let suppressNavClick = false;

      // Expose to inline onclick
      window.navigateTo = function(url) {
        if (suppressNavClick) return;
        window.location.href = url;
      };

      function setActive(activeIdx) {
        items.forEach((btn, i) => {
          btn.classList.toggle('active', i === activeIdx);
        });
      }

      function centerNavItem(btn, behavior = 'smooth') {
        const desired =
          btn.offsetLeft + (btn.offsetWidth / 2) - (nav.clientWidth / 2);

        const maxScroll = nav.scrollWidth - nav.clientWidth;
        const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(desired, maxScroll));

        nav.scrollTo({
          left: clamped,
          behavior
        });
      }

      // Center active item on load
      const initiallyActive = nav.querySelector('.group-nav-item.active');
      if (initiallyActive) centerNavItem(initiallyActive, 'auto');

      // --- Drag to scroll ---
      (function enableDragScroll(container) {

        let isDown = false;
        let startX = 0;
        let startScrollLeft = 0;
        let dragging = false;

        const DRAG_THRESHOLD = 10;

        container.style.cursor = 'grab';

        container.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
          if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && e.button !== 0) return;

          isDown = true;
          dragging = false;
          startX = e.clientX;
          startScrollLeft = container.scrollLeft;

          container.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
          if (!isDown) return;

          const dx = e.clientX - startX;

          if (!dragging && Math.abs(dx) > DRAG_THRESHOLD) {
            dragging = true;
          }

          if (dragging) {
            if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
            container.scrollLeft = startScrollLeft - dx;
          }

        }, { passive: false });

        container.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';

          if (dragging) {
            suppressNavClick = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
              suppressNavClick = false;
            }, 0);
          }
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';
        });

      })(nav);

    });
  </script>

<li><strong>Cookie:</strong> _gid</li>

<li><strong>Description:</strong> Registers a unique ID that is used to generate statistical data on how the visitor uses the website.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="manageOption">

  function clearFIlters() {
    const clearFiltersButton = document.getElementById('clearFilters');
    clearFiltersButton.click();
  }
  document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    var showcase = new Splide('#showcase', {
      type: 'loop',
      perPage: 1.2,
      gap: '20px',
      focus: 'center',
      arrows: false,
      pagination: false,
      trimSpace: false,
      autoHeight: true,
      clones: 2,
      // Auto scroll
      autoplay: true,
      interval: 5000,
      pauseOnHover: true,
      pauseOnFocus: true,
    });
    showcase.mount();

    var eventShowcase = new Splide('#eventShowcase', {
      type: 'slide',
      perPage: 2.2,
      gap: '20px',
      focus: 'left',
      arrows: false,
      pagination: false,
      trimSpace: false,
      autoHeight: true,
      clones: 2,
      breakpoints: {
        1280: {
          perPage: 2.2,
        },
        964: {
          perPage: 1.2,
        }
      }
    });
    eventShowcase.mount();
  });

  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
    const searchBar = document.querySelector("#filterControlls-search");
    const filterControls = document.querySelector("#filterControlls");
    window.addEventListener("scroll", function() {
      let filterTop = filterControls.getBoundingClientRect().top;
      // searchBar.classList.remove("hidden");
      if (filterTop <= 35) {
        // searchBar.classList.add("hidden");
      }
    });
  });
</script>
<script>
document.querySelectorAll('.drag-scroll').forEach((slider) => {
  let isDown = false;
  let isDragging = false;
  let startX = 0;
  let scrollLeft = 0;
  const dragThreshold = 6;

  // Desktop mouse drag only
  slider.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
    if (e.button !== 0) return;

    isDown = true;
    isDragging = false;
    startX = e.pageX;
    scrollLeft = slider.scrollLeft;

    slider.classList.add('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
  });

  window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
    if (!isDown) return;

    const dx = e.pageX - startX;

    if (Math.abs(dx) > dragThreshold) {
      isDragging = true;
    }

    if (isDragging) {
      e.preventDefault();
      slider.scrollLeft = scrollLeft - dx;
    }
  });

  slider.querySelectorAll('a').forEach((link) => {
    link.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (isDragging) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
      }
    });

    link.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
    });
  });
});
</script>

<!-- If the developer is debugging in Chrome -->
<script>
GlobalDebuggingInChrome = false;



//Clears Footer Contact Select dropdown
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');
    cinemaSelect.selectedIndex = 0;
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');

  if (!cinemaSelect) return;

  cinemaSelect.addEventListener('change', function () {
    const siteCode = this.value;

    if (siteCode !== '') {
      window.location.href = '/company/contact?site=' + encodeURIComponent(siteCode);
    }
  });
});
</script>

function handleLatLonPosition(pos) {
	var requestURL = '/api/internalapi/?action=setLatLon&lat=' + pos.coords.latitude + '&lng=' + pos.coords.longitude;
	console.log('API: ' + requestURL);

	var request = $.ajax({
    url: requestURL,
    type: "get",
		async: false,
    data: {}
  });

  request.done(function(response, textStatus, jqXHR){
    console.log('Lat/Lon Stored');
  });
}

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.relative').forEach(function(alert) {
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      alert.classList.remove('hidden');
      alert.classList.add('block');
    });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.close-alert').forEach(function(button) {
    button.addEventListener('click', function() {
      const alertDiv = button.closest('.relative');
      if (alertDiv) {
        alertDiv.classList.remove('block');
        alertDiv.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {

      const nav = document.getElementById('mobileSliderNav');
      if (!nav) return;

      const items = Array.from(nav.querySelectorAll('.group-nav-item'));
      const sections = items.map(btn => document.getElementById(btn.dataset.target));

      let suppressNavClick = false;

      // Expose to inline onclick
      window.navigateTo = function(url) {
        if (suppressNavClick) return;
        window.location.href = url;
      };

      function setActive(activeIdx) {
        items.forEach((btn, i) => {
          btn.classList.toggle('active', i === activeIdx);
        });
      }

      function centerNavItem(btn, behavior = 'smooth') {
        const desired =
          btn.offsetLeft + (btn.offsetWidth / 2) - (nav.clientWidth / 2);

        const maxScroll = nav.scrollWidth - nav.clientWidth;
        const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(desired, maxScroll));

        nav.scrollTo({
          left: clamped,
          behavior
        });
      }

      // Center active item on load
      const initiallyActive = nav.querySelector('.group-nav-item.active');
      if (initiallyActive) centerNavItem(initiallyActive, 'auto');

      // --- Drag to scroll ---
      (function enableDragScroll(container) {

        let isDown = false;
        let startX = 0;
        let startScrollLeft = 0;
        let dragging = false;

        const DRAG_THRESHOLD = 10;

        container.style.cursor = 'grab';

        container.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
          if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && e.button !== 0) return;

          isDown = true;
          dragging = false;
          startX = e.clientX;
          startScrollLeft = container.scrollLeft;

          container.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
          if (!isDown) return;

          const dx = e.clientX - startX;

          if (!dragging && Math.abs(dx) > DRAG_THRESHOLD) {
            dragging = true;
          }

          if (dragging) {
            if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
            container.scrollLeft = startScrollLeft - dx;
          }

        }, { passive: false });

        container.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';

          if (dragging) {
            suppressNavClick = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
              suppressNavClick = false;
            }, 0);
          }
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';
        });

      })(nav);

    });
  </script>

  }

    var showcase = new Splide('#showcase', {
      type: 'loop',
      perPage: 1.2,
      gap: '20px',
      focus: 'center',
      arrows: false,
      pagination: false,

      // Auto scroll

    });

      type: 'slide',

        }
      }
    });
    eventShowcase.mount();
  });

  document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
    const searchBar = document.querySelector("#filterControlls-search");
    const filterControls = document.querySelector("#filterControlls");

</script>
<script>
document.querySelectorAll('.drag-scroll').forEach((slider) => {
  let isDown = false;
  let isDragging = false;
  let startX = 0;
  let scrollLeft = 0;
  const dragThreshold = 6;

  // Desktop mouse drag only
  slider.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
    if (e.button !== 0) return;

    isDown = true;
    isDragging = false;
    startX = e.pageX;
    scrollLeft = slider.scrollLeft;

    slider.classList.add('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
  });

  window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
    if (!isDown) return;

    const dx = e.pageX - startX;

    if (Math.abs(dx) > dragThreshold) {
      isDragging = true;
    }

    if (isDragging) {
      e.preventDefault();
      slider.scrollLeft = scrollLeft - dx;
    }
  });

  slider.querySelectorAll('a').forEach((link) => {
    link.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (isDragging) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
      }
    });

    link.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
GlobalDebuggingInChrome = false;



//Clears Footer Contact Select dropdown
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');
    cinemaSelect.selectedIndex = 0;
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');

  if (!cinemaSelect) return;

  cinemaSelect.addEventListener('change', function () {
    const siteCode = this.value;

    if (siteCode !== '') {
      window.location.href = '/company/contact?site=' + encodeURIComponent(siteCode);
    }
  });
});
</script>

function handleLatLonPosition(pos) {
	var requestURL = '/api/internalapi/?action=setLatLon&lat=' + pos.coords.latitude + '&lng=' + pos.coords.longitude;
	console.log('API: ' + requestURL);

	var request = $.ajax({
    url: requestURL,
    type: "get",
		async: false,
    data: {}
  });

  request.done(function(response, textStatus, jqXHR){
    console.log('Lat/Lon Stored');
  });
}

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.relative').forEach(function(alert) {
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      alert.classList.remove('hidden');
      alert.classList.add('block');
    });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.close-alert').forEach(function(button) {
    button.addEventListener('click', function() {
      const alertDiv = button.closest('.relative');
      if (alertDiv) {
        alertDiv.classList.remove('block');
        alertDiv.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {

      const nav = document.getElementById('mobileSliderNav');
      if (!nav) return;

      const items = Array.from(nav.querySelectorAll('.group-nav-item'));
      const sections = items.map(btn => document.getElementById(btn.dataset.target));

      let suppressNavClick = false;

      // Expose to inline onclick
      window.navigateTo = function(url) {
        if (suppressNavClick) return;
        window.location.href = url;
      };

      function setActive(activeIdx) {
        items.forEach((btn, i) => {
          btn.classList.toggle('active', i === activeIdx);
        });
      }

      function centerNavItem(btn, behavior = 'smooth') {
        const desired =
          btn.offsetLeft + (btn.offsetWidth / 2) - (nav.clientWidth / 2);

        const maxScroll = nav.scrollWidth - nav.clientWidth;
        const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(desired, maxScroll));

        nav.scrollTo({
          left: clamped,
          behavior
        });
      }

      // Center active item on load
      const initiallyActive = nav.querySelector('.group-nav-item.active');
      if (initiallyActive) centerNavItem(initiallyActive, 'auto');

      // --- Drag to scroll ---
      (function enableDragScroll(container) {

        let isDown = false;
        let startX = 0;
        let startScrollLeft = 0;
        let dragging = false;

        const DRAG_THRESHOLD = 10;

        container.style.cursor = 'grab';

        container.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
          if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && e.button !== 0) return;

          isDown = true;
          dragging = false;
          startX = e.clientX;
          startScrollLeft = container.scrollLeft;

          container.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
          if (!isDown) return;

          const dx = e.clientX - startX;

          if (!dragging && Math.abs(dx) > DRAG_THRESHOLD) {
            dragging = true;
          }

          if (dragging) {
            if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
            container.scrollLeft = startScrollLeft - dx;
          }

        }, { passive: false });

        container.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';

          if (dragging) {
            suppressNavClick = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
              suppressNavClick = false;
            }, 0);
          }
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';
        });

      })(nav);

    });
  </script>

<h3>Other</h3>
<p>Other uncategorized cookies are those that are being analyzed and have not been classified into a category as yet.</p>

  }

    });

        }
      }
    });
    eventShowcase.mount();
  });

</script>
<script>
document.querySelectorAll('.drag-scroll').forEach((slider) => {
  let isDown = false;
  let isDragging = false;
  let startX = 0;
  let scrollLeft = 0;
  const dragThreshold = 6;

  // Desktop mouse drag only
  slider.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
    if (e.button !== 0) return;

    isDown = true;
    isDragging = false;
    startX = e.pageX;
    scrollLeft = slider.scrollLeft;

    slider.classList.add('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
  });

  window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
    if (!isDown) return;

    const dx = e.pageX - startX;

    if (Math.abs(dx) > dragThreshold) {
      isDragging = true;
    }

    if (isDragging) {
      e.preventDefault();
      slider.scrollLeft = scrollLeft - dx;
    }
  });

  slider.querySelectorAll('a').forEach((link) => {
    link.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (isDragging) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
      }
    });

    link.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
GlobalDebuggingInChrome = false;



//Clears Footer Contact Select dropdown
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');
    cinemaSelect.selectedIndex = 0;
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');

  if (!cinemaSelect) return;

  cinemaSelect.addEventListener('change', function () {
    const siteCode = this.value;

    if (siteCode !== '') {
      window.location.href = '/company/contact?site=' + encodeURIComponent(siteCode);
    }
  });
});

function handleLatLonPosition(pos) {
	var requestURL = '/api/internalapi/?action=setLatLon&lat=' + pos.coords.latitude + '&lng=' + pos.coords.longitude;
	console.log('API: ' + requestURL);

	var request = $.ajax({
    url: requestURL,
    type: "get",
		async: false,
    data: {}
  });

  request.done(function(response, textStatus, jqXHR){
    console.log('Lat/Lon Stored');
  });
}

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.relative').forEach(function(alert) {
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      alert.classList.remove('hidden');
      alert.classList.add('block');
    });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.close-alert').forEach(function(button) {
    button.addEventListener('click', function() {
      const alertDiv = button.closest('.relative');
      if (alertDiv) {
        alertDiv.classList.remove('block');
        alertDiv.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {

      const nav = document.getElementById('mobileSliderNav');
      if (!nav) return;

      const items = Array.from(nav.querySelectorAll('.group-nav-item'));
      const sections = items.map(btn => document.getElementById(btn.dataset.target));

      let suppressNavClick = false;

      // Expose to inline onclick
      window.navigateTo = function(url) {
        if (suppressNavClick) return;
        window.location.href = url;
      };

      function setActive(activeIdx) {
        items.forEach((btn, i) => {
          btn.classList.toggle('active', i === activeIdx);
        });
      }

      function centerNavItem(btn, behavior = 'smooth') {
        const desired =
          btn.offsetLeft + (btn.offsetWidth / 2) - (nav.clientWidth / 2);

        const maxScroll = nav.scrollWidth - nav.clientWidth;
        const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(desired, maxScroll));

        nav.scrollTo({
          left: clamped,
          behavior
        });
      }

      // Center active item on load
      const initiallyActive = nav.querySelector('.group-nav-item.active');
      if (initiallyActive) centerNavItem(initiallyActive, 'auto');

      // --- Drag to scroll ---
      (function enableDragScroll(container) {

        let isDown = false;
        let startX = 0;
        let startScrollLeft = 0;
        let dragging = false;

        const DRAG_THRESHOLD = 10;

        container.style.cursor = 'grab';

        container.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
          if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && e.button !== 0) return;

          isDown = true;
          dragging = false;
          startX = e.clientX;
          startScrollLeft = container.scrollLeft;

          container.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
          if (!isDown) return;

          const dx = e.clientX - startX;

          if (!dragging && Math.abs(dx) > DRAG_THRESHOLD) {
            dragging = true;
          }

          if (dragging) {
            if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
            container.scrollLeft = startScrollLeft - dx;
          }

        }, { passive: false });

        container.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';

          if (dragging) {
            suppressNavClick = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
              suppressNavClick = false;
            }, 0);
          }
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';
        });

      })(nav);

    });
  </script>

<script>
document.querySelectorAll('.drag-scroll').forEach((slider) => {
  let isDown = false;
  let isDragging = false;
  let startX = 0;
  let scrollLeft = 0;
  const dragThreshold = 6;

  // Desktop mouse drag only
  slider.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
    if (e.button !== 0) return;

    isDown = true;
    isDragging = false;
    startX = e.pageX;
    scrollLeft = slider.scrollLeft;

    slider.classList.add('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
  });

  window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
    if (!isDown) return;

    const dx = e.pageX - startX;

    if (Math.abs(dx) > dragThreshold) {
      isDragging = true;
    }

    if (isDragging) {
      e.preventDefault();
      slider.scrollLeft = scrollLeft - dx;
    }
  });

  slider.querySelectorAll('a').forEach((link) => {
    link.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (isDragging) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
      }
    });

    link.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
    });
  });
});
</script>

//Clears Footer Contact Select dropdown
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');
    cinemaSelect.selectedIndex = 0;
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');

  if (!cinemaSelect) return;

  cinemaSelect.addEventListener('change', function () {
    const siteCode = this.value;

    if (siteCode !== '') {
      window.location.href = '/company/contact?site=' + encodeURIComponent(siteCode);
    }
  });
});

function handleLatLonPosition(pos) {
	var requestURL = '/api/internalapi/?action=setLatLon&lat=' + pos.coords.latitude + '&lng=' + pos.coords.longitude;
	console.log('API: ' + requestURL);

	var request = $.ajax({
    url: requestURL,
    type: "get",
		async: false,
    data: {}
  });

  request.done(function(response, textStatus, jqXHR){
    console.log('Lat/Lon Stored');
  });
}

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.relative').forEach(function(alert) {
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      alert.classList.remove('hidden');
      alert.classList.add('block');
    });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.close-alert').forEach(function(button) {
    button.addEventListener('click', function() {
      const alertDiv = button.closest('.relative');
      if (alertDiv) {
        alertDiv.classList.remove('block');
        alertDiv.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {

      const nav = document.getElementById('mobileSliderNav');
      if (!nav) return;

      const items = Array.from(nav.querySelectorAll('.group-nav-item'));
      const sections = items.map(btn => document.getElementById(btn.dataset.target));

      let suppressNavClick = false;

      // Expose to inline onclick
      window.navigateTo = function(url) {
        if (suppressNavClick) return;
        window.location.href = url;
      };

      function setActive(activeIdx) {
        items.forEach((btn, i) => {
          btn.classList.toggle('active', i === activeIdx);
        });
      }

      function centerNavItem(btn, behavior = 'smooth') {
        const desired =
          btn.offsetLeft + (btn.offsetWidth / 2) - (nav.clientWidth / 2);

        const maxScroll = nav.scrollWidth - nav.clientWidth;
        const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(desired, maxScroll));

        nav.scrollTo({
          left: clamped,
          behavior
        });
      }

      // Center active item on load
      const initiallyActive = nav.querySelector('.group-nav-item.active');
      if (initiallyActive) centerNavItem(initiallyActive, 'auto');

      // --- Drag to scroll ---
      (function enableDragScroll(container) {

        let isDown = false;
        let startX = 0;
        let startScrollLeft = 0;
        let dragging = false;

        const DRAG_THRESHOLD = 10;

        container.style.cursor = 'grab';

        container.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
          if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && e.button !== 0) return;

          isDown = true;
          dragging = false;
          startX = e.clientX;
          startScrollLeft = container.scrollLeft;

          container.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
          if (!isDown) return;

          const dx = e.clientX - startX;

          if (!dragging && Math.abs(dx) > DRAG_THRESHOLD) {
            dragging = true;
          }

          if (dragging) {
            if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
            container.scrollLeft = startScrollLeft - dx;
          }

        }, { passive: false });

        container.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';

          if (dragging) {
            suppressNavClick = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
              suppressNavClick = false;
            }, 0);
          }
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';
        });

      })(nav);

    });
  </script>

<script>
document.querySelectorAll('.drag-scroll').forEach((slider) => {
  let isDown = false;
  let isDragging = false;
  let startX = 0;
  let scrollLeft = 0;
  const dragThreshold = 6;

  // Desktop mouse drag only
  slider.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
    if (e.button !== 0) return;

    isDown = true;
    isDragging = false;
    startX = e.pageX;
    scrollLeft = slider.scrollLeft;

    slider.classList.add('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
  });

  window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
    if (!isDown) return;

    const dx = e.pageX - startX;

    if (Math.abs(dx) > dragThreshold) {
      isDragging = true;
    }

    if (isDragging) {
      e.preventDefault();
      slider.scrollLeft = scrollLeft - dx;
    }
  });

  slider.querySelectorAll('a').forEach((link) => {
    link.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (isDragging) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
      }
    });

    link.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
    });
  });
});
</script>

//Clears Footer Contact Select dropdown
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');
    cinemaSelect.selectedIndex = 0;
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');

  if (!cinemaSelect) return;

  cinemaSelect.addEventListener('change', function () {
    const siteCode = this.value;

    if (siteCode !== '') {
      window.location.href = '/company/contact?site=' + encodeURIComponent(siteCode);
    }
  });
});

function handleLatLonPosition(pos) {
	var requestURL = '/api/internalapi/?action=setLatLon&lat=' + pos.coords.latitude + '&lng=' + pos.coords.longitude;
	console.log('API: ' + requestURL);

	var request = $.ajax({
    url: requestURL,
    type: "get",
		async: false,
    data: {}
  });

  request.done(function(response, textStatus, jqXHR){
    console.log('Lat/Lon Stored');
  });
}

<script>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
  document.querySelectorAll('.relative').forEach(function(alert) {
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      alert.classList.remove('hidden');
      alert.classList.add('block');
    });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('.close-alert').forEach(function(button) {
    button.addEventListener('click', function() {
      const alertDiv = button.closest('.relative');
      if (alertDiv) {
        alertDiv.classList.remove('block');
        alertDiv.classList.add('hidden');
      }
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {

      const nav = document.getElementById('mobileSliderNav');
      if (!nav) return;

      const items = Array.from(nav.querySelectorAll('.group-nav-item'));
      const sections = items.map(btn => document.getElementById(btn.dataset.target));

      let suppressNavClick = false;

      // Expose to inline onclick
      window.navigateTo = function(url) {
        if (suppressNavClick) return;
        window.location.href = url;
      };

      function setActive(activeIdx) {
        items.forEach((btn, i) => {
          btn.classList.toggle('active', i === activeIdx);
        });
      }

      function centerNavItem(btn, behavior = 'smooth') {
        const desired =
          btn.offsetLeft + (btn.offsetWidth / 2) - (nav.clientWidth / 2);

        const maxScroll = nav.scrollWidth - nav.clientWidth;
        const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(desired, maxScroll));

        nav.scrollTo({
          left: clamped,
          behavior
        });
      }

      // Center active item on load
      const initiallyActive = nav.querySelector('.group-nav-item.active');
      if (initiallyActive) centerNavItem(initiallyActive, 'auto');

      // --- Drag to scroll ---
      (function enableDragScroll(container) {

        let isDown = false;
        let startX = 0;
        let startScrollLeft = 0;
        let dragging = false;

        const DRAG_THRESHOLD = 10;

        container.style.cursor = 'grab';

        container.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
          if (e.pointerType === 'mouse' && e.button !== 0) return;

          isDown = true;
          dragging = false;
          startX = e.clientX;
          startScrollLeft = container.scrollLeft;

          container.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
          if (!isDown) return;

          const dx = e.clientX - startX;

          if (!dragging && Math.abs(dx) > DRAG_THRESHOLD) {
            dragging = true;
          }

          if (dragging) {
            if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault();
            container.scrollLeft = startScrollLeft - dx;
          }

        }, { passive: false });

        container.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';

          if (dragging) {
            suppressNavClick = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
              suppressNavClick = false;
            }, 0);
          }
        });

        container.addEventListener('pointercancel', () => {
          isDown = false;
          container.style.cursor = 'grab';
        });

      })(nav);

    });
  </script>

<script>
document.querySelectorAll('.drag-scroll').forEach((slider) => {
  let isDown = false;
  let isDragging = false;
  let startX = 0;
  let scrollLeft = 0;
  const dragThreshold = 6;

  // Desktop mouse drag only
  slider.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
    if (e.button !== 0) return;

    isDown = true;
    isDragging = false;
    startX = e.pageX;
    scrollLeft = slider.scrollLeft;

    slider.classList.add('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
  });

  window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
    isDown = false;
    slider.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    slider.style.cursor = '';
  });

  slider.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
    if (!isDown) return;

    const dx = e.pageX - startX;

    if (Math.abs(dx) > dragThreshold) {
      isDragging = true;
    }

    if (isDragging) {
      e.preventDefault();
      slider.scrollLeft = scrollLeft - dx;
    }
  });

  slider.querySelectorAll('a').forEach((link) => {
    link.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      if (isDragging) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
      }
    });

    link.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
    });
  });
});
</script>

<!-- If the developer is debugging in Chrome -->
<script>
GlobalDebuggingInChrome = false;



//Clears Footer Contact Select dropdown
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');
    cinemaSelect.selectedIndex = 0;
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
  const cinemaSelect = document.getElementById('conactlocalCinema');

  if (!cinemaSelect) return;

  cinemaSelect.addEventListener('change', function () {
    const siteCode = this.value;

    if (siteCode !== '') {
      window.location.href = '/company/contact?site=' + encodeURIComponent(siteCode);
    }
  });
});
</script>
